23:["$","p",null,{"children":["This tutorial presents ",["$","em",null,{"children":"Pauli Correlation Encoding"}]," (PCE) ",["$","a",null,{"href":"#references","className":"text-link-primary no-underline hover:underline inline","ref":"$undefined","children":"[1]"}],", an approach designed to encode optimization problems into qubits with greater efficiency for quantum computation. PCE maps classical variables in optimization problems to multi-body Pauli-matrix correlations, resulting in a polynomial compression of the problem's space requirements. By employing PCE, the number of qubits needed for encoding is reduced, making it particularly advantageous for near-term quantum devices with limited qubit resources. Furthermore, it is analytically demonstrated that PCE inherently mitigates barren plateaus, offering super-polynomial resilience against this phenomenon. This built-in feature enables unprecedented performance in quantum optimization solvers."],"className":"group paragraph-el text-text-primary mx-0"}]
